The gage height associated with the measurement made on May 13, 1942, was revised on July 9, 2025, based on discovery of a database entry error. The discharge measurements made on June 28, 2006 and September 8, 2011 were deemed to be in error at the time of their review and were removed from publication on Aug. 27, 2025. The gage height associated with the peak discharge of Mar. 18, 1936 was deemed unreliable and removed from publication on Aug. 27, 2025.

CHENANGO RIVER AT SHERBURNE NY, USGS site 01505000 (USGS-01505000), stream in Chenango County, New York, draining 263 square miles. Daily means from 1938-05-31 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
